# #1073 OmniRoute row dispositions: 2026-10-07 through 2026-10-15

## Inventory and method

Source index: `/tmp/omni-oct-2026-10-07-31.json`, read only as JSON data. The inclusive UTC interval contains **665 unique merged PRs**; each record includes `headRefOid` and `mergeCommit.oid`, retained below. The search denominator of 665 matches the saved prior independent search total.

Changed-file metadata was fetched in bounded GraphQL batches of 20. All **665/665** records have complete changed-file paths, each list matching GitHub `totalCount`; paths are unique within each PR. **12** lists exceeded the first 100 paths and were completed with **58** additional 100-file pages. No pagination gaps remain. The exact full path inventory is printed in every row.

Disposition rules: new provider additions and sign-in are assigned to the Provider Sign-in lane; rows whose complete changed paths are exclusively docs/tests/CI/changelog are recorded as non-product surfaces; source changes without a defensible source-to-local behavior match remain explicitly unresolved. Titles are labels, not proof of applicability. Existing dashboard source changes remain eligible and are not dismissed by title. This is not a selection slate.

## Focused source-to-local checks (five cases)

1. **#15647, Claude tool-result cache markers — confirmed applicable gap.** Pinned merge `8ee4929b5302d5350a7c7710adad1ee2886bc34f`; final source `open-sse/translator/helpers/claudeHelper.ts` hoists nested `tool_result.content[].cache_control` to its parent, strips inner markers, and enforces the four-marker limit. Local `open-sse/translator/formats/claude.js` counts and caps root message blocks but has no corresponding nested-result hoist; [tests/unit/claude-cache-control.test.js](../../../tests/unit/claude-cache-control.test.js) exercises anchoring/capping, not nested tool-result markers. Narrow acceptance: synthetic nested marker is hoisted, inner markers removed, existing outer marker preserved, and request-wide cap maintained. No duplicate implementation was added.
2. **#15457, Claude Code bridge tool history — partial/uncertain.** Pinned merge `967f4c17e2c073553603488b4575e1c77d22cec8`; final source maps OpenAI assistant tool calls and tool results to Claude-compatible turns. The local general translator has tool-call and tool-result conversion in `open-sse/translator/request/openai-to-claude.js`; related local pairing coverage exists in [tests/translator/claude-kiro-direct.test.js](../../../tests/translator/claude-kiro-direct.test.js), but no equivalent bridge-specific source/module or acceptance was found. Keep unresolved pending bridge caller equivalence; acceptance should preserve ordered assistant calls, IDs, and corresponding result turns for the bridge.
3. **#15158, `responses` supported endpoint — unresolved, partial local analogue.** Pinned merge `be7ee7bb5bec11221dbd93d2c13c3b87aeb1f527`; final source adds `responses` to the general supported-endpoint vocabulary. The local [Copilot endpoint contract test](../../../tests/unit/copilot-endpoint-contract-1073.test.js) covers a provider-specific Responses contract, but this checkout has no corresponding general model-supported-endpoint module or mapped acceptance. Do not count endpoint routing elsewhere as proof of equivalent model metadata. Acceptance should cover normalization, persisted model endpoint values, and existing chat/modality behavior.
4. **#15132, Codex live model inventory — unresolved, local caller not mapped.** Pinned merge `884c7c429a7059a3c11d2590576d54c249b8a49b`; final `src/app/api/providers/[id]/models/discovery/codex.ts` gives live inventory authority for model existence while merging capacity conservatively with the local catalog. This checkout has no Codex discovery module at that path and no test mapped to that merge contract; generic Codex catalog and routing tests do not establish equivalent discovery behavior. Keep unresolved pending comparison of the local model-discovery caller and its refresh tests.
5. **#15624, Claude quota warning/reset — unresolved.** Pinned merge `ca464676383b64c7d29852bc514dfd4c5d677280`; final source changes quota-cache/auth reset handling. Local quota selection and reset tracking exist in `src/sse/services/auth.js` and `src/shared/services/quotaAutoPing.js`; [quota-auto-ping.test.js](../../../tests/unit/quota-auto-ping.test.js) and [provider-quota-transport.test.js](../../../tests/unit/provider-quota-transport.test.js) cover adjacent behavior but not the Claude-scoped reset contract. The source change does not map directly to local policy. Do not transplant behavior without a caller-level contract comparison; no duplicate implementation was added.
